# Pair judgments — pairs, rows 1400–1499

[Population and scope](../pairs.md) · [Relationship guide](../README.md) · [Scientific account](../../BWP-S.md)

Native row order is retained. A negative or unresolved record remains a substantive judgment; a directed record creates no unlisted reverse. Shared passages link to their complete exact values.
